[u0003 | 00:00:16.109-00:00:32.370 | DM] As the days are growing long in the desert of Dunmar, the heat reaching its hottest point, the dry desert terrain around you longing for the relief that the summer thunderstorms that come following the {{music}} monsoons bring.

[u0007 | 00:00:47.790-00:01:08.880 | DM] Public square of Karawa, to the sounds of hope: the chatter of people mixing mud into bricks to dry in the sun, the bang of hammers as rubble is broken into smaller pieces to be cleared or reused for new construction, the regular click of shovels as dirt is dug for building materials.
